=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
There are N houses at points (X_1,Y_1),\ldots,(X_N,Y_N) on a two-dimensional plane.
Initially, Santa Claus is at point (S_x,S_y). He will act according to the sequence (D_1,C_1),\ldots,(D_M,C_M) as follows:

- For i=1,2,\ldots,M in order, he moves as follows:
- Let (x,y) be the point where he currently is.
- If D_i is U, move in a straight line from (x,y) to (x,y+C_i).
- If D_i is D, move in a straight line from (x,y) to (x,y-C_i).
- If D_i is L, move in a straight line from (x,y) to (x-C_i,y).
- If D_i is R, move in a straight line from (x,y) to (x+C_i,y).





Find the point where he is after completing all actions, and the number of distinct houses he passed through or arrived at during his actions. If the same house is passed multiple times, it is only counted once.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N M S_x S_y
X_1 Y_1
\vdots
X_N Y_N
D_1 C_1
\vdots
D_M C_M

Output

Let (X,Y) be the point where he is after completing all actions, and C be the number of distinct houses passed through or arrived at. Print X,Y,C in this order separated by spaces.

Constraints


- 1 \leq N \leq 2\times 10^5
- 1 \leq M \leq 2\times 10^5
- -10^9 \leq X_i,Y_i \leq 10^9
- The pairs (X_i,Y_i) are distinct.
- -10^9 \leq S_x,S_y \leq 10^9
- There is no house at (S_x,S_y).
- Each D_i is one of U, D, L, R.
- 1 \leq C_i \leq 10^9
- All input numbers are integers.

Sample Input 1

3 4 3 2
2 2
3 3
2 1
L 2
D 1
R 1
U 2

Sample Output 1

2 3 2

Santa Claus behaves as follows:


- D_1= L, so he moves from (3,2) to (3-2,2) in a straight line. During this, he passes through the house at (2,2).
- D_2= D, so he moves from (1,2) to (1,2-1) in a straight line.
- D_3= R, so he moves from (1,1) to (1+1,1) in a straight line. During this, he passes through the house at (2,1).
- D_4= U, so he moves from (2,1) to (2,1+2) in a straight line. During this, he passes through the house at (2,2), but it has already been passed.

The number of houses he passed or arrived during his actions is 2.

Sample Input 2

1 3 0 0
1 1
R 1000000000
R 1000000000
R 1000000000

Sample Output 2

3000000000 0 0

Be careful with overflow.

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

   vertical_segments = []  # list of (x, y1, y2)
   For each move:
      D = next token bytes; C = int.
      if D == b'U':
          y2 = y + C
          vertical_segments.append((x, y, y2))
          y = y2
      elif D == b'D':
          y2 = y - C
          vertical_segments.append((x, y2, y))  # y2 < y
          y = y2
      elif D == b'L':
          x2 = x - C
          l = x2; r = x
          horizontal_events.append((l, 1, y))  # add
          horizontal_events.append((r+1, -1, y))  # remove
          x = x2
      elif D == b'R':
          x2 = x + C
          l = x; r = x2
          horizontal_events.append((l, 1, y))

Implementation details:

```
import sys
from bisect import bisect_right

def main():
    data = sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()
    it = iter(data)
    N = int(next(it))
    M = int(next(it))
    Sx = int(next(it))
    Sy = int(next(it))
    houses = []
    for _ in range(N):
        x = int(next(it)); y = int(next(it))
        houses.append((x,y))
    x = Sx; y = Sy
    horiz_events = []
    vert_segments = []
    for _ in range(M):
        D = next(it)
        C = int(next(it))
        if D == b'U':
            y2 = y + C
            vert_segments.append((x, y, y2))
            y = y2
        elif D == b'D':
            y2 = y - C
            vert_segments.append((x, y2, y))
            y = y2
        elif D == b'L':
            x2 = x - C
            l = x2; r = x
            horiz_events.append((l, 1, y))
            horiz_events.append((r + 1, -1, y))
            x = x2
        else: # R
            x2 = x + C
            l = x; r = x2
            horiz_events.append((l, 1, y))
            horiz_events.append((r + 1, -1, y))
            x = x2
    final_x, final_y = x, y

    # build vertical_by_x
    vert_segments.sort(key=lambda t: (t[0], t[1]))
    vertical_by_x = {}
    i = 0
    V = len(vert_segments)
    while i < V:
        x0 = vert_segments[i][0]
        cur_l = vert_segments[i][1]
        cur_r = vert_segments[i][2]
        i += 1
        intervals = []
        while i < V and vert_segments[i][0] == x0:
            l = vert_segments[i][1]
            r = vert_segments[i][2]
            if l <= cur_r + 1:
                if r > cur_r:
                    cur_r = r
            else:
                intervals.append((cur_l, cur_r))
                cur_l, cur_r = l, r
            i += 1
        intervals.append((cur_l, cur_r))
        starts = [iv[0] for iv in intervals]
        ends = [iv[1] for iv in intervals]
        vertical_by_x[x0] = (starts, ends)

    houses.sort(key=lambda p: p[0])
    horiz_events.sort(key=lambda e: e[0])
    active = {}
    ans = 0
    eidx = 0
    E = len(horiz_events)
    i = 0
    while i < N:
        px = houses[i][0]
        while eidx < E and horiz_events[eidx][0] <= px:
            _, delta, y = horiz_events[eidx]
            if delta == 1:
                active[y] = active.get(y, 0) + 1
            else:
                cnt = active[y] - 1
                if cnt:
                    active[y] = cnt
                else:
                    del active[y]
            eidx += 1
        while i < N and houses[i][0] == px:
            py = houses[i][1]
            if active.get(py, 0) > 0:
                ans += 1
            else:
                v = vertical_by_x.get(px)
                if v is not None:
                    starts, ends = v
                    idx = bisect_right(starts, py) - 1
                    if idx >= 0 and ends[idx] >= py:
                        ans += 1
            i += 1

    print(final_x, final_y, ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```
